# Document 2 — Original units, NORMALIZED to Cameo's scale

_AUTO-GENERATED by `tools/balance/synthesize_reference.py` from [`ORIGINAL_UNITS_RAW.md`](ORIGINAL_UNITS_RAW.md) (Document 1). Spec: [`BALANCE_SYNTHESIS.md`](BALANCE_SYNTHESIS.md) §15.2. Do not hand-edit._

Every source unit with each stat put on **Cameo's** scale, so a source unit and a Cameo unit can be read in the same column. The anchor is the basic rifleman: **20,000 HP / 100 credits / speed 60** — the `scout` class spec in `class_anchors.json`.

Each source is normalized to **its own** rifle first (§15.6.2): the sources do not share a scale — Romanov's Vengeance runs a 12,500 HP rifle, Mental Omega 205, vanilla RA2 125 — so an un-normalized comparison between them is meaningless.

⚠ **No damage or Versus column, deliberately.** §15.4: raw Versus is not comparable across sources and must never be numerically averaged; only a warhead's relative ORDER carries identity. That is `tools/reference/aggregate_archetype.py`'s job.
